```
31	export function haversineKm(lat1: number, lng1: number, lat2: number, lng2: number): number {
32	  const R = 6371
33	  const dLat = (lat2 - lat1) * Math.PI / 180
34	  const dLng = (lng2 - lng1) * Math.PI / 180
35	  const a =
36	    Math.sin(dLat / 2) ** 2 +
37	    Math.cos(lat1 * Math.PI / 180) * Math.cos(lat2 * Math.PI / 180) *
38	    Math.sin(dLng / 2) ** 2
39	  return R * 2 * Math.atan2(Math.sqrt(a), Math.sqrt(1 - a))
40	}
41	
42	export function nearestTown(lat: number, lng: number): string {
43	  let best = AP_TOWNS[0]
44	  let bestDist = haversineKm(lat, lng, best.lat, best.lng)
45	  for (const town of AP_TOWNS) {
46	    const d = haversineKm(lat, lng, town.lat, town.lng)
47	    if (d < bestDist) { bestDist = d; best = town }
48	  }
49	  return best.name
50	}
51	
52	export function formatDistance(km: number): string {
53	  if (km < 1) return `${Math.round(km * 1000)} m`
54	  if (km < 10) return `${km.toFixed(1)} km`
55	  return `${Math.round(km)} km`
56	}
```

```
30	export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
31	  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
32	  const q = searchParams.get('q') ?? ''
33	  const method = searchParams.get('method') ?? ''
34	  const category = searchParams.get('category') ?? ''
35	
36	  const { data: produce } = await supabase
37	    .from('produce_listings')
38	    .select('*')
39	    .eq('status', 'available')
40	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
41	
42	  let filtered = produce ?? []
43	
44	  if (q) {
45	    const query = q.toLowerCase()
46	    filtered = filtered.filter(
47	      (p) =>
48	        p.name?.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
49	        p.variety?.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
50	        p.description?.toLowerCase().includes(query)
51	    )
52	  }
53	
54	  if (method && method !== 'all') {
55	    filtered = filtered.filter((p) =>
56	      p.method?.toLowerCase().includes(method.toLowerCase())
57	    )
58	  }
59	
60	  if (category && category !== 'all' && CATEGORY_KEYWORDS[category]) {
61	    const keywords = CATEGORY_KEYWORDS[category]
62	    filtered = filtered.filter((p) =>
63	      keywords.some((kw) => p.name?.toLowerCase().includes(kw))
64	    )
65	  }
66	
67	  if (!filtered.length) return NextResponse.json([])
68	
69	  const farmerIds = [...new Set(filtered.map((p) => p.farmer_id))]
70	
71	  const { data: farmers } = await supabase
72	    .from('farmers')
73	    .select('id, name, village, slug, phone, method, pickup_locations, pickup_slots')
74	    .in('id', farmerIds)
75	    .eq('active', true)
76	
77	  const farmerMap = Object.fromEntries((farmers ?? []).map((f) => [f.id, f]))
78	
79	  const result = filtered
80	    .map((p) => ({ ...p, farmer: farmerMap[p.farmer_id] ?? null }))
81	    .filter((p) => p.farmer !== null)
82	
83	  return NextResponse.json(result)
84	}
85
```

```
1	'use client'
2	
3	import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react'
4	
5	type Suggestion = {
6	  name: string
7	  subtitle: string
8	  lat: number
9	  lng: number
10	}
11	
12	// AP + Telangana bbox: min_lon,min_lat,max_lon,max_lat
13	const BBOX = '76.0,12.0,85.5,20.5'
14	
15	// States we allow in results
16	const ALLOWED_STATES = ['andhra pradesh', 'telangana']
17	
18	// eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-explicit-any
19	function parsePhoton(data: any): Suggestion[] {
20	  const seen = new Set<string>()
21	  const results: Suggestion[] = []
22	
23	  for (const feature of data?.features ?? []) {
24	    const p = feature?.properties ?? {}
25	    const coords: [number, number] = feature?.geometry?.coordinates ?? [0, 0]
26	    const lng = coords[0]
27	    const lat = coords[1]
28	
29	    if (!lat || !lng) continue
30	
31	    // Filter to AP + Telangana only
32	    const state: string = (p.state ?? '').toLowerCase()
33	    if (state && !ALLOWED_STATES.some((s) => state.includes(s))) continue
34	
35	    // Best name for this result
36	    const name: string = (
37	      p.name || p.city || p.town || p.village || p.hamlet || p.suburb || p.county || ''
38	    ).trim()
39	    if (!name) continue
40	
41	    const key = name.toLowerCase()
42	    if (seen.has(key)) continue
43	    seen.add(key)
44	
45	    const parts: string[] = []
46	    const district = p.county || p.district || p.state_district
47	    if (district && district.toLowerCase() !== key) parts.push(district)
48	    if (p.state) parts.push(p.state)
49	
50	    results.push({ name, subtitle: parts.join(', '), lat, lng })
51	  }
52	
53	  return results
54	}
55	
56	async function fetchSuggestions(q: string, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<Suggestion[]> {
57	  const url = new URL('https://photon.komoot.io/api/')
58	  url.searchParams.set('q', q)
59	  url.searchParams.set('limit', '8')
60	  url.searchParams.set('bbox', BBOX)
61	  url.searchParams.set('lang', 'en')
62	
63	  const res = await fetch(url.toString(), { signal })
64	  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`)
65	  const json = await res.json()
66	  return parsePhoton(json)
67	}
68	
69	export default function LocationSearch({
70	  placeholder = 'Search city, town or village... / పట్టణం వెతకండి',
71	  onSelect,
72	  initialValue = '',
73	}: {
74	  placeholder?: string
75	  onSelect: (lat: number, lng: number, name: string) => void
76	  initialValue?: string
77	}) {
78	  const [query, setQuery]             = useState(initialValue)
79	  const [suggestions, setSuggestions] = useState<Suggestion[]>([])
80	  const [loading, setLoading]         = useState(false)
81	  const [showDrop, setShowDrop]       = useState(false)
82	  const [noResults, setNoResults]     = useState(false)
83	  const [dropUp, setDropUp]           = useState(false)
84	  const abortRef     = useRef<AbortController | null>(null)
85	  const timerRef     = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null)
86	  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
87	  const inputRef     = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null)
88	
89	  const doSearch = async (q: string) => {
90	    abortRef.current?.abort()
91	    const clean = q.trim()
92	    if (clean.length < 2) {
93	      setSuggestions([])
94	      setNoResults(false)
95	      setLoading(false)
96	      setShowDrop(false)
97	      return
98	    }
99	    abortRef.current = new AbortController()
100	    setLoading(true)
101	    setNoResults(false)
102	    try {
103	      const results = await fetchSuggestions(clean, abortRef.current.signal)
104	      setSuggestions(results)
105	      setNoResults(results.length === 0)
106	      setShowDrop(true)
107	    } catch (err: unknown) {
108	      if ((err as Error).name !== 'AbortError') {
109	        setSuggestions([])
110	        setNoResults(true)
111	      }
112	    } finally {
113	      setLoading(false)
114	    }
115	  }
116	
117	  const handleChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
118	    const val = e.target.value
119	    setQuery(val)
120	    if (timerRef.current) clearTimeout(timerRef.current)
121	    timerRef.current = setTimeout(() => doSearch(val), 350)
122	    if (val.trim().length < 2) {
123	      setSuggestions([])
124	      setNoResults(false)
125	      setShowDrop(false)
126	    }
127	  }
128	
129	  const handleSelect = (s: Suggestion) => {
130	    setQuery(s.name)
131	    setSuggestions([])
132	    setShowDrop(false)
133	    setNoResults(false)
134	    onSelect(s.lat, s.lng, s.name)
135	  }
136	
137	  const handleClear = () => {
138	    setQuery('')
139	    setSuggestions([])
140	    setShowDrop(false)
141	    setNoResults(false)
142	    abortRef.current?.abort()
143	  }
144	
145	  // Flip dropdown upward when the keyboard shrinks the visual viewport and
146	  // leaves insufficient space below the input to show results
147	  useEffect(() => {
148	    const vv = window.visualViewport
149	    if (!vv) return
150	    const update = () => {
151	      if (!inputRef.current) return
152	      const rect = inputRef.current.getBoundingClientRect()
153	      // Pixels available between the bottom of the input and the top of the keyboard
154	      const spaceBelow = vv.height + vv.offsetTop - rect.bottom
155	      setDropUp(spaceBelow < 220)
156	    }
157	    vv.addEventListener('resize', update)
158	    vv.addEventListener('scroll', update)
159	    return () => {
160	      vv.removeEventListener('resize', update)
161	      vv.removeEventListener('scroll', update)
162	    }
163	  }, [])
164	
165	  // Close on outside click
166	  useEffect(() => {
167	    const handler = (e: MouseEvent) => {
168	      if (containerRef.current && !containerRef.current.contains(e.target as Node)) {
169	        setShowDrop(false)
170	      }
171	    }
172	    document.addEventListener('mousedown', handler)
173	    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handler)
174	  }, [])
175	
176	  useEffect(() => () => { abortRef.current?.abort() }, [])
177	
178	  return (
179	    <div ref={containerRef} className="relative">
180	      {/* Input */}
181	      <div className="relative">
182	        <svg
183	          className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 pointer-events-none"
184	          width="17" height="17" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none"
185	          stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="2.5" strokeLinecap="round"
186	        >
187	          <circle cx="11" cy="11" r="8" />
188	          <path d="m21 21-4.35-4.35" />
189	        </svg>
190	        <input
191	          ref={inputRef}
192	          type="text"
193	          value={query}
194	          onChange={handleChange}
195	          onFocus={() => { if (suggestions.length > 0) setShowDrop(true) }}
196	          placeholder={placeholder}
197	          autoComplete="off"
198	          spellCheck={false}
199	          className="w-full border-2 border-gray-200 rounded-xl pl-9 pr-9 py-3 text-base focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none bg-white"
200	        />
201	        {loading && (
202	          <span className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none">
203	            <span className="animate-spin inline-block w-4 h-4 border-2 border-green-600 border-t-transparent rounded-full" />
204	          </span>
205	        )}
206	        {!loading && query && (
207	          <button
208	            type="button"
209	            onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); handleClear() }}
210	            className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 text-xl leading-none"
211	          >
212	            ×
213	          </button>
214	        )}
215	      </div>
216	
217	      {/* Suggestions dropdown */}
218	      {showDrop && suggestions.length > 0 && (
219	        <div className={`absolute z-30 left-0 right-0 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl shadow-xl overflow-hidden max-h-64 overflow-y-auto ${dropUp ? 'bottom-full mb-1' : 'top-full mt-1'}`}>
220	          {suggestions.map((s, i) => (
221	            <button
222	              key={i}
223	              type="button"
224	              onMouseDown={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); handleSelect(s) }}
225	              className="w-full text-left px-4 py-3 hover:bg-green-50 active:bg-green-100 border-b border-gray-100 last:border-0"
226	            >
227	              <p className="font-semibold text-sm text-gray-900 leading-snug">📍 {s.name}</p>
228	              {s.subtitle && (
229	                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">{s.subtitle}</p>
230	              )}
231	            </button>
232	          ))}
233	        </div>
234	      )}
235	
236	      {/* No results */}
237	      {showDrop && noResults && !loading && (
238	        <div className={`absolute z-30 left-0 right-0 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-xl shadow-xl px-4 py-4 text-center ${dropUp ? 'bottom-full mb-1' : 'top-full mt-1'}`}>
239	          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-600">
240	            No results / ఫలితాలు లేవు
241	          </p>
242	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1 leading-snug">
243	            Try the full name or a nearby larger town<br />
244	            పూర్తి పేరు లేదా దగ్గరలోని పెద్ద పట్టణం వెతకండి
245	          </p>
246	        </div>
247	      )}
248	    </div>
249	  )
250	}
251
```

```
3	import { useState } from 'react'
4	import Link from 'next/link'
5	import { useLang } from '@/lib/LanguageContext'
6	import { useCart } from '@/components/consumer/Cart'
7	
8	type ProduceItem = {
9	  id: string
10	  name: string
11	  variety?: string
12	  emoji?: string
13	  farmer_id: string
14	  price_tier_1_price?: number
15	  price_tier_1_qty?: number
16	  price_tier_2_price?: number
17	  price_tier_2_qty?: number
18	  price_tier_3_price?: number

20	  stock_qty?: number
21	  method?: string
22	}
23	
24	type Farmer = {
25	  id: string
26	  slug: string
27	  name: string
28	  village?: string
29	  phone?: string
30	  pickup_locations?: string[]
31	  rating_avg?: number

33	
34	export default function BrowseProduceTab({
35	  produce,
36	  farmers,
37	}: {
38	  produce: Record<string, unknown>[]
39	  farmers: Record<string, unknown>[]
40	}) {
41	  const { tx } = useLang()
42	  const { addItem, cart } = useCart()
43	  const [search, setSearch] = useState('')
44	  const [addedIds, setAddedIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set())
45	
46	  const list = produce as ProduceItem[]
47	  const farmerList = farmers as Farmer[]
48	
49	  const getFarmer = (farmerId: string) => farmerList.find((f) => f.id === farmerId)
50	
51	  const filtered = list.filter((p) => {
52	    if (!search.trim()) return true
53	    const q = search.toLowerCase()
54	    return (
55	      p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
56	      (p.variety ?? '').toLowerCase().includes(q)
57	    )
58	  })
59	
60	  const handleAddToCart = (item: ProduceItem) => {
61	    const farmer = getFarmer(item.farmer_id)
62	    if (!farmer?.phone) return
63	
64	    addItem({
65	      listingId: item.id,
66	      name: item.name,
67	      variety: item.variety,
68	      emoji: item.emoji,
69	      pricePerKg: item.price_tier_1_price,
70	      priceTier1Qty: item.price_tier_1_qty,
71	      priceTier1Price: item.price_tier_1_price,
72	      priceTier2Qty: item.price_tier_2_qty,
73	      priceTier2Price: item.price_tier_2_price,
74	      priceTier3Price: item.price_tier_3_price,
75	      unit: item.unit,
76	      stockQty: item.stock_qty,
77	      farmerId: farmer.id,
78	      farmerName: farmer.name,
79	      farmerPhone: farmer.phone,
80	      farmerVillage: farmer.village ?? '',
81	      farmerSlug: farmer.slug,
82	      farmerPickupLocations: farmer.pickup_locations,
83	    })
84	
85	    setAddedIds((prev) => new Set(prev).add(item.id))
86	    setTimeout(() => {
87	      setAddedIds((prev) => {
88	        const next = new Set(prev)
89	        next.delete(item.id)
90	        return next
91	      })
92	    }, 2000)
93	  }
94	
95	  if (list.length === 0) {
96	    return (
97	      <div className="text-center py-10 text-gray-400 text-sm">{tx.noProduceFound}</div>
98	    )
99	  }
100	
101	  return (
102	    <div className="space-y-3">
103	      <input
104	        type="text"
105	        value={search}
106	        onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
107	        placeholder={tx.searchProducePlaceholder}
108	        className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none bg-white"
109	      />
110	
111	      {filtered.length === 0 ? (
112	        <div className="text-center py-8 text-gray-400 text-sm">{tx.noProduceFound}</div>
113	      ) : (
114	        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
115	          {filtered.map((item) => {
116	            const farmer = getFarmer(item.farmer_id)
117	            if (!farmer) return null
118	            const added = addedIds.has(item.id)
119	            const inCart = !!cart[item.id]
120	            const canCart = !!item.price_tier_1_price && !!farmer.phone
121	
122	            return (
123	              <div key={item.id} className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
124	                <Link href={`/farmer/${farmer.slug}#produce`} className="block p-3">
125	                  <div className="text-3xl mb-2">{item.emoji ?? '🌿'}</div>
126	                  <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 leading-tight">{item.name}</h3>
127	                  {item.variety && (
128	                    <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 mt-0.5 truncate">{item.variety}</p>
129	                  )}
130	                  <p className="text-xs text-green-700 font-semibold mt-1 truncate">
131	                    {farmer.name}
132	                    {farmer.village ? ` · ${farmer.village}` : ''}
133	                  </p>
134	                  {farmer.rating_avg ? (
135	                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5">
136	                      <span className="text-yellow-400 text-xs">★</span>
137	                      <span className="text-xs text-gray-500">{farmer.rating_avg.toFixed(1)}</span>
138	                    </div>
139	                  ) : null}
140	                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-2">
141	                    {item.price_tier_1_price ? (
142	                      <span className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">
143	                        ₹{item.price_tier_1_price}
144	                        <span className="text-xs font-normal text-gray-400">/{item.unit ?? 'kg'}</span>
145	                      </span>
146	                    ) : (
147	                      <span />
148	                    )}
149	                    {item.method === 'natural' && (
150	                      <span className="bg-green-100 text-green-800 text-[10px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full">
151	                        Natural
152	                      </span>
153	                    )}
154	                  </div>
155	                  {item.stock_qty !== undefined && item.stock_qty > 0 && (
156	                    <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-1">
157	                      {item.stock_qty} {item.unit ?? 'kg'} left
158	                    </p>
159	                  )}
160	                </Link>
161	
162	                {canCart && (
163	                  <div className="px-3 pb-3">
164	                    <button
165	                      onClick={() => handleAddToCart(item)}
166	                      className={`w-full py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-colors ${
167	                        added
168	                          ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800'
169	                          : inCart
170	                          ? 'bg-green-50 text-green-700 border border-green-300'
171	                          : 'bg-green-700 text-white active:bg-green-800'
172	                      }`}
173	                    >
174	                      {added ? tx.addedToCart : inCart ? `In cart ✓` : tx.addToCart}
175	                    </button>
176	                  </div>
177	                )}
178	              </div>
179	            )
180	          })}
181	        </div>
182	      )}
183	    </div>
184	  )
185	}
186
```

```
1	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
2	import { notFound } from 'next/navigation'
3	import RegionTopBar from '@/components/region/RegionTopBar'
4	import RegionHero from '@/components/region/RegionHero'
5	import RegionContent from '@/components/region/RegionContent'
6	
7	export async function generateMetadata({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }) {
8	  const { slug } = await params
9	  const { data: region } = await supabase
10	    .from('regions')
11	    .select('name, district')
12	    .eq('slug', slug)
13	    .single()
14	
15	  if (!region) return { title: 'Region not found' }
16	
17	  return {
18	    title: `Natural Farmers in ${region.name} — YourFamilyFarmer`,
19	    description: `Find natural farmers near ${region.name}, ${region.district}. Buy fresh produce directly with no middlemen.`,
20	  }
21	}
22	
23	export default async function RegionPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ slug: string }> }) {
24	  const { slug } = await params
25	
26	  const { data: region } = await supabase
27	    .from('regions')
28	    .select('*')
29	    .eq('slug', slug)
30	    .eq('active', true)
31	    .single()
32	
33	  if (!region) notFound()
34	
35	  const { data: farmers } = await supabase
36	    .from('farmers')
37	    .select('*')
38	    .eq('region_slug', slug)
39	    .eq('active', true)
40	    .order('created_at', { ascending: true })
41	
42	  const farmerIds = (farmers ?? []).map((f) => f.id)
43	
44	  const { data: produce } = farmerIds.length
45	    ? await supabase
46	        .from('produce_listings')
47	        .select('*')
48	        .in('farmer_id', farmerIds)
49	        .eq('status', 'available')
50	        .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
51	    : { data: [] }
52	
53	  return (
54	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50">
55	      <RegionTopBar region={region} />
56	      <RegionHero region={region} farmerCount={farmers?.length ?? 0} produceCount={produce?.length ?? 0} />
57	      <RegionContent farmers={farmers ?? []} produce={produce ?? []} />
58	    </main>
59	  )
60	}
61
```

```
1	'use client'
2	
3	import { useState } from 'react'
4	import { useLang } from '@/lib/LanguageContext'
5	import RegionMap from './RegionMap'
6	import FarmersTab from './tabs/FarmersTab'
7	import BrowseProduceTab from './tabs/BrowseProduceTab'
8	import AddFarmerTab from './tabs/AddFarmerTab'
9	import { CartFab } from '@/components/consumer/Cart'
10	
11	const CATEGORY_MAP: Record<string, string[]> = {
12	  Vegetables: ['Tomato', 'Brinjal', 'Drumstick', 'Bitter Gourd', 'Raw Banana'],
13	  Fruits: ['Mango', 'Banana', 'Papaya', 'Guava'],
14	  'Leafy greens': ['Palakura', 'Spinach', 'Methi', 'Coriander'],
15	}
16	
17	export default function RegionContent({
18	  farmers,
19	  produce,
20	}: {
21	  farmers: Record<string, unknown>[]
22	  produce: Record<string, unknown>[]
23	}) {
24	  const { tx } = useLang()
25	  const [activeFilter, setActiveFilter] = useState('All')
26	  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState(0)
27	  const [highlightedFarmerId, setHighlightedFarmerId] = useState<string | null>(null)
28	
29	  const FILTERS = [tx.all, tx.vegetables, tx.fruits, tx.leafyGreens, tx.naturalOnly]
30	  const TABS = [tx.farmers, tx.browseProduce, tx.addFarmer]
31	
32	  // Map filter label back to English key for category lookup
33	  const filterKeyMap: Record<string, string> = {
34	    [tx.all]: 'All',
35	    [tx.vegetables]: 'Vegetables',
36	    [tx.fruits]: 'Fruits',
37	    [tx.leafyGreens]: 'Leafy greens',
38	    [tx.naturalOnly]: 'Natural only',
39	  }
40	
41	  const filterKey = filterKeyMap[activeFilter] ?? activeFilter
42	
43	  const filteredProduce = produce.filter((p) => {
44	    if (filterKey === 'All') return true
45	    if (filterKey === 'Natural only') return p.method === 'natural'
46	    const names = CATEGORY_MAP[filterKey] ?? []
47	    return names.some((n) => (p.name as string).includes(n))
48	  })
49	
50	  const filteredFarmerIds = new Set(filteredProduce.map((p) => p.farmer_id))
51	  const filteredFarmers = filterKey === 'All'
52	    ? farmers
53	    : farmers.filter((f) => filteredFarmerIds.has(f.id))
54	
55	  return (
56	    <div>
57	      {/* Filter chips */}
58	      <div className="bg-white border-b border-gray-100 px-4 py-3">
59	        <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto scrollbar-hide">
60	          {FILTERS.map((filter) => (
61	            <button
62	              key={filter}
63	              onClick={() => setActiveFilter(filter)}
64	              className={`flex-shrink-0 px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full text-sm font-semibold border transition-all ${
65	                activeFilter === filter
66	                  ? 'bg-green-700 text-white border-green-700'
67	                  : 'bg-white text-gray-600 border-gray-200'
68	              }`}
69	            >
70	              {filter}
71	            </button>
72	          ))}
73	        </div>
74	      </div>
75	
76	      {/* SVG Map */}
77	      <RegionMap
78	        farmers={filteredFarmers}
79	        highlightedFarmerId={highlightedFarmerId}
80	        onPinClick={setHighlightedFarmerId}
81	      />
82	
83	      {/* Tabs */}
84	      <div className="sticky top-0 z-40 bg-white border-b border-gray-200">
85	        <div className="flex">
86	          {TABS.map((tab, i) => (
87	            <button
88	              key={tab}
89	              onClick={() => setActiveTab(i)}
90	              className={`flex-1 py-3 text-xs font-semibold border-b-2 transition-colors ${
91	                activeTab === i
92	                  ? 'border-green-700 text-green-700'
93	                  : 'border-transparent text-gray-500'
94	              }`}
95	            >
96	              {tab}
97	            </button>
98	          ))}
99	        </div>
100	      </div>
101	
102	      <div className="px-4 py-4">
103	        {activeTab === 0 && (
104	          <FarmersTab
105	            farmers={filteredFarmers}
106	            produce={produce}
107	            highlightedFarmerId={highlightedFarmerId}
108	          />
109	        )}
110	        {activeTab === 1 && <BrowseProduceTab produce={filteredProduce} farmers={farmers} />}
111	        {activeTab === 2 && <AddFarmerTab farmerCount={farmers.length} />}
112	      </div>
113	
114	      <CartFab />
115	    </div>
116	  )
117	}
118
```

```
1	'use client'
2	
3	import { useState } from 'react'
4	import Link from 'next/link'
5	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
6	import { useLang } from '@/lib/LanguageContext'
7	import LanguageToggle from '@/components/LanguageToggle'
8	
9	type Order = {
10	  id: string
11	  produce_name: string | null
12	  quantity: number | null
13	  unit: string | null
14	  total_price: number | null
15	  pickup_location: string | null
16	  status: 'pending' | 'approved' | 'declined'
17	  payment_method: string | null
18	  payment_status: string | null
19	  created_at: string
20	  farmer?: {
21	    name: string
22	    slug: string
23	    village: string
24	    upi_id: string | null
25	  } | null
26	}
27	
28	export default function ConsumerOrdersPage() {
29	  const { tx } = useLang()
30	  const [phone, setPhone]     = useState('')
31	  const [orders, setOrders]   = useState<Order[]>([])
32	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
33	  const [searched, setSearched] = useState(false)
34	  const [error, setError]     = useState('')
35	
36	  const digits = phone.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-10)
37	  const canSearch = digits.length === 10
38	
39	  const handleSearch = async () => {
40	    if (!canSearch) return
41	    setLoading(true)
42	    setError('')
43	    setSearched(false)
44	
45	    const { data, error: err } = await supabase
46	      .from('orders')
47	      .select('id, produce_name, quantity, unit, total_price, pickup_location, status, payment_method, payment_status, created_at, farmer_id')
48	      .eq('buyer_phone', digits)
49	      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
50	      .limit(50)
51	
52	    if (err) {
53	      setError(err.message)
54	      setLoading(false)
55	      return
56	    }
57	
58	    // Fetch farmer names in one query
59	    const farmerIds = [...new Set((data ?? []).map((o) => o.farmer_id).filter(Boolean))]
60	    let farmerMap: Record<string, { name: string; slug: string; village: string; upi_id: string | null }> = {}
61	
62	    if (farmerIds.length > 0) {
63	      const { data: farmers } = await supabase

67	      farmerMap = Object.fromEntries(

72	    const result: Order[] = (data ?? []).map((o) => ({

82	  const statusColor = (s: string) =>

103	  return (

137	              maxLength={10}

143	          <button

147	          >

173	              const upiLink = needsPayment
```

```
85	export default function ConsumerPage() {
86	  const [available, setAvailable]     = useState<ProduceListing[]>([])
87	  const [comingSoon, setComingSoon]   = useState<ProduceListing[]>([])
88	  const [filtered, setFiltered]       = useState<ProduceListing[]>([])
89	  const [search, setSearch]           = useState('')
90	  const [method, setMethod]           = useState('all')
91	  const [category, setCategory]       = useState('all')
92	  const [loading, setLoading]         = useState(true)
93	  const [farmerCount, setFarmerCount] = useState(0)
94	  const searchAbortRef = useRef<AbortController | null>(null)
95	
96	  // Consumer location
97	  const [consumerLat, setConsumerLat]           = useState<number | null>(null)
98	  const [consumerLng, setConsumerLng]           = useState<number | null>(null)
99	  const [consumerLocationName, setConsumerLocationName] = useState('')
100	  const [showLocationSheet, setShowLocationSheet]       = useState(false)
101	  const [distanceFilter, setDistanceFilter]             = useState<number | null>(null)
102	
103	  const fetchData = useCallback(async (silent = false) => {
104	    if (!silent) setLoading(true)
105	    try {
106	      const [avRes, csRes] = await Promise.all([
107	        fetch('/api/produce', { cache: 'no-store' }),
108	        fetch('/api/produce?status=coming_soon', { cache: 'no-store' }),
109	      ])
110	      const av: ProduceListing[] = await avRes.json().catch(() => [])
111	      const cs: ProduceListing[] = await csRes.json().catch(() => [])
112	      const avArr = Array.isArray(av) ? av : []
113	      const csArr = Array.isArray(cs) ? cs : []
114	      setAvailable(avArr)
115	      setFiltered(avArr)
116	      setComingSoon(csArr)
117	      setFarmerCount(new Set(avArr.map((p) => p.farmer_id)).size)
118	    } catch { /* silent */ }
119	    if (!silent) setLoading(false)
120	  }, [])
121	
122	  useEffect(() => {
123	    fetchData()
124	    // Background refreshes — no skeleton flash
125	    const onVisible = () => { if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') fetchData(true) }
126	    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisible)
127	    const channel = supabase
128	      .channel('produce_listings_consumer')
129	      .on('postgres_changes', { event: '*', schema: 'public', table: 'produce_listings' }, () => fetchData(true))
130	      .subscribe()
131	    return () => {
132	      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', onVisible)
133	      supabase.removeChannel(channel)
134	    }
135	  }, [fetchData])
136	
137	  const doSearch = useCallback(async () => {
138	    if (!search && method === 'all' && category === 'all') {
139	      setFiltered(available)
140	      return
141	    }
142	    searchAbortRef.current?.abort()
143	    searchAbortRef.current = new AbortController()
144	    try {
145	      const p = new URLSearchParams()
146	      if (search)              p.set('q', search)
147	      if (method !== 'all')    p.set('method', method)
148	      if (category !== 'all')  p.set('category', category)
149	      const res = await fetch(`/api/produce/search?${p}`, { cache: 'no-store', signal: searchAbortRef.current.signal })
150	      const data = await res.json().catch(() => [])
151	      setFiltered(Array.isArray(data) ? data : [])
152	    } catch (e) {
153	      if ((e as Error).name !== 'AbortError') { /* silent */ }
154	    }
155	  }, [search, method, category, available])
156	
157	  useEffect(() => {
158	    const t = setTimeout(doSearch, 300)
159	    return () => clearTimeout(t)
160	  }, [doSearch])
161	
162	  // Load consumer location from localStorage
163	  useEffect(() => {
164	    const lat  = localStorage.getItem('yff_consumer_lat')
165	    const lng  = localStorage.getItem('yff_consumer_lng')
166	    const name = localStorage.getItem('yff_consumer_location_name')
167	    if (lat && lng) {
168	      setConsumerLat(Number(lat))
169	      setConsumerLng(Number(lng))
170	      setConsumerLocationName(name ?? '')
171	    } else if (!localStorage.getItem('yff_location_prompted')) {
172	      setShowLocationSheet(true)
173	    }
174	  }, [])
175	
176	  const saveConsumerLocation = useCallback((lat: number, lng: number, name: string) => {
177	    setConsumerLat(lat)
178	    setConsumerLng(lng)
179	    setConsumerLocationName(name)
180	    localStorage.setItem('yff_consumer_lat', String(lat))
181	    localStorage.setItem('yff_consumer_lng', String(lng))
182	    localStorage.setItem('yff_consumer_location_name', name)
183	    localStorage.setItem('yff_location_prompted', '1')
184	    setShowLocationSheet(false)
185	  }, [])
186	
187	  // Sort + distance-filter produce
188	  const displayItems = useMemo(() => {
189	    type WithDist = ProduceListing & { distKm: number | null }
190	    const withDist: WithDist[] = filtered.map((item) => ({
191	      ...item,
192	      distKm: (consumerLat && consumerLng && item.farmer?.lat && item.farmer?.lng)
193	        ? haversineKm(consumerLat, consumerLng, item.farmer.lat, item.farmer.lng)
194	        : null,
195	    }))
196	    if (!consumerLat || !consumerLng) return withDist
197	    let result = withDist
198	    if (distanceFilter) {
199	      result = result.filter((i) => i.distKm !== null && i.distKm <= distanceFilter)
200	    }
201	    return result.sort((a, b) => {
202	      if (a.distKm === null && b.distKm === null) return 0
203	      if (a.distKm === null) return 1
204	      if (b.distKm === null) return -1
205	      return a.distKm - b.distKm
206	    })
207	  }, [filtered, consumerLat, consumerLng, distanceFilter])
208	
209	  return (
210	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-12">
211	      <GlobalNav activeTab="consumer" />
212

306	      </div>
307	
308	      {/* ── Distance filter chips (only when location set) ── */}
309	      {consumerLat && consumerLng && (
310	        <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto mt-3 px-4">
311	          <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto scrollbar-hide pb-1">
312	            {([null, 5, 10, 25] as const).map((d) => (
313	              <button
314	                key={d ?? 'all'}
315	                onClick={() => setDistanceFilter(d)}
316	                className={`flex-shrink-0 px-4 py-2 rounded-full text-sm font-semibold border transition-all ${
317	                  distanceFilter === d
318	                    ? 'bg-green-700 text-white border-green-700'
319	                    : 'bg-white text-gray-700 border-gray-200'
320	                }`}
321	              >
322	                {d === null ? 'All / అన్నీ' : `< ${d} km`}
323	              </button>
324	            ))}
325	          </div>
326	        </div>
327	      )}
328	
329	      {/* ── Available now ────────────────────── */}
330	      <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 mt-6">
331	        <div className="flex items-end justify-between mb-4">
332	          <div>
333	            <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-gray-900">Available now / ఇప్పుడు అందుబాటులో</h2>
334	            <p className="text-green-700 font-semibold text-sm leading-tight">
335	              Fresh produce from farms nearby / తాజా పంట
336	            </p>
337	          </div>
338	          {!loading && (
339	            <span className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-400">
340	              {displayItems.length} items / వస్తువులు
341	            </span>
342	          )}
343	        </div>
344	
345	        {loading ? (
346	          <LoadingSkeleton />
347	        ) : displayItems.length === 0 ? (
348	          distanceFilter ? (
349	            <DistanceEmptyState km={distanceFilter} onClear={() => setDistanceFilter(null)} />
350	          ) : (
351	            <EmptyState />
352	          )
353	        ) : (
354	          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
355	            {displayItems.map((item) => (
356	              <ProduceCard key={item.id} item={item} distanceKm={'distKm' in item ? (item as ProduceListing & { distKm: number | null }).distKm : null} />
357	            ))}
358	          </div>
359	        )}
360	      </div>
361	
362	      {/* ── Coming soon ──────────────────────── */}
363	      {!loading && comingSoon.length > 0 && (
364	        <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto px-4 mt-10">
365	          <div className="mb-4">
366	            <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-gray-900">Coming soon / త్వరలో వస్తుంది</h2>
367	            <p className="text-amber-600 font-semibold text-sm">
368	              Reserve early / ముందుగానే రిజర్వ్
369	            </p>
370	          </div>
371	          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
372	            {comingSoon.map((item) => (
373	              <ComingSoonCard key={item.id} item={item} />
374	            ))}
375	          </div>
376	        </div>
377	      )}
378	
379	      {/* ── Demand intent banner ─────────────── */}
380	      <DemandIntentBanner />
381	
382	      {/* ── Floating cart button ─────────────── */}
383	      <CartFab />
384	
385	      {/* ── Location bottom sheet ─────────────── */}
386	      {showLocationSheet && (
387	        <LocationBottomSheet
388	          onSet={saveConsumerLocation}
389	          onClose={() => {
390	            localStorage.setItem('yff_location_prompted', '1')
391	            setShowLocationSheet(false)
392	          }}
393	        />
394	      )}
395	    </main>
396	  )
397	}
398	
399	/* ─── Produce card ──────────────────────────────────────── */
400	function ProduceCard({ item, distanceKm }: { item: ProduceListing; distanceKm?: number | null }) {
401	  const emoji      = item.emoji ?? '🌿'
402	  const emojiBg    = EMOJI_BG[emoji] ?? 'bg-green-50'
403	  const method     = item.method?.toLowerCase() ?? 'natural'
404	  const badge      = METHOD_STYLE[method]  ?? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800'
405	  const badgeLabel = METHOD_LABEL[method] ?? 'Natural'
406	  const farmer     = item.farmer
407	  const farmerHref = farmer ? `/farmer/${farmer.slug}` : '#'
408	  const unit       = item.unit || 'kg'
409	
410	  const { tx } = useLang()
411	  const { cart, addItem, setQty } = useCart()
412	  const inCart = cart[item.id]
413	
414	  const canAdd = !!farmer && !!farmer.phone
415	
416	  const [liveStock, setLiveStock] = useState<number | null>(item.stock_qty ?? null)
417	  const [stockMsg, setStockMsg]   = useState('')
418	  const [adding, setAdding]       = useState(false)
419	
420	  useEffect(() => { setLiveStock(item.stock_qty ?? null) }, [item.stock_qty])
421	
422	  const isOutOfStock = liveStock !== null && liveStock <= 0
423	  const atMax        = liveStock !== null && inCart != null && inCart.qty >= liveStock
424	
425	  const handleAdd = async () => {
426	    if (!farmer) return
427	    setAdding(true)
428	    setStockMsg('')
429	
430	    const { data } = await supabase
431	      .from('produce_listings')
432	      .select('stock_qty')
433	      .eq('id', item.id)
434	      .single()
435	
436	    const fresh = data?.stock_qty ?? null
437	    if (fresh !== null) setLiveStock(fresh)
438	    setAdding(false)
439	
440	    if (fresh !== null) {
441	      if (fresh <= 0) {
442	        setStockMsg('Out of stock / అయిపోయింది')
443	        return
444	      }
445	      const curQty = inCart?.qty ?? 0
446	      if (curQty >= fresh) {
447	        setStockMsg(`Maximum available / గరిష్ట పరిమాణం: ${fresh} ${unit}`)
448	        return
449	      }
450	      if (curQty + 1 > fresh) {
451	        setStockMsg(`Stock updated / స్టాక్ మారింది, only ${fresh} ${unit} available`)
452	        return
453	      }
454	    }
455	
456	    addItem({
457	      listingId: item.id,
458	      name: item.name,
459	      variety: item.variety,
460	      emoji: item.emoji,
461	      unit,
462	      stockQty: fresh != null ? fresh : (item.stock_qty ?? undefined),
463	      pricePerKg: item.price_tier_1_price,
464	      priceTier1Qty: item.price_tier_1_qty,
465	      priceTier1Price: item.price_tier_1_price,
466	      priceTier2Qty: item.price_tier_2_qty,
467	      priceTier2Price: item.price_tier_2_price,
468	      priceTier3Price: item.price_tier_3_price,
469	      farmerId: farmer.id,
470	      farmerName: farmer.name,
471	      farmerPhone: farmer.phone,
472	      farmerVillage: farmer.village,
473	      farmerSlug: farmer.slug,
474	      farmerPickupLocations: farmer.pickup_locations ?? [],
475	      farmerPickupSlots: farmer.pickup_slots ?? null,
476	    }, 1)
477	  }
478	
479	  const handleInc = () => {
480	    if (liveStock !== null && inCart.qty >= liveStock) {
481	      setStockMsg(`Maximum available / గరిష్ట పరిమాణం: ${liveStock} ${unit}`)
482	      return
483	    }
484	    setStockMsg('')
485	    setQty(item.id, inCart.qty + 1)
486	  }
487	
488	  return (
489	    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex flex-col">
490	      {/* Image (or emoji fallback) */}
491	      <Link href={farmerHref}>
492	        {item.image_url ? (
493	          <div className="bg-gray-100 aspect-[4/3] overflow-hidden">
494	            <img
495	              src={item.image_url}
496	              alt={item.name}
497	              loading="lazy"
498	              className="w-full h-full object-cover"
499	            />
500	          </div>
501	        ) : (
502	          <div className={`${emojiBg} flex items-center justify-center py-7`}>
503	            <span className="text-5xl">{emoji}</span>
504	          </div>
505	        )}
506	      </Link>
507	
508	      <div className="p-3 flex flex-col flex-1 gap-1">
509	        {/* Name + variety */}
510	        <Link href={farmerHref}>
511	          <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base leading-tight">{item.name}</h3>
512	          {item.variety && (
513	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-0.5">{item.variety}</p>
514	          )}
515	        </Link>
516	
517	        {/* Farmer info */}
518	        {farmer && (
519	          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
520	            <span className="text-xs text-gray-500 leading-snug">
521	              🧑‍🌾 {farmer.name}<br />
522	              📍 {farmer.village}
523	            </span>
524	            <Link
525	              href={farmerHref}
526	              className="flex-shrink-0 text-[11px] font-semibold text-green-700 border border-green-300 rounded-full px-2.5 py-1 bg-green-50 active:bg-green-100 whitespace-nowrap"
527	            >
528	              {tx.viewFarmerProfile}
529	            </Link>
530	          </div>
531	        )}
532	
533	        {/* Distance badge */}
534	        {distanceKm != null && (
535	          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-blue-700 bg-blue-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full self-start">
536	            📍 {formatDistance(distanceKm)} away
537	          </span>
538	        )}
539	
540	        {/* Price + method badge */}
541	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-1">
542	          <span className="text-green-700 font-black text-lg">
543	            {item.price_tier_1_price ? `₹${item.price_tier_1_price}` : '—'}
544	            <span className="text-xs font-normal text-gray-400">/{unit}</span>
545	          </span>
546	          <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${badge}`}>
547	            {badgeLabel}
548	          </span>
549	        </div>
550	
551	        {/* Freshness badge */}
552	        {item.harvest_date && <FreshnessBadge harvestDate={item.harvest_date} />}
553	
554	        {/* Stock display */}
555	        {liveStock != null && (
556	          <p className={`text-xs font-medium ${liveStock === 0 ? 'text-red-500' : 'text-gray-400'}`}>
557	            {liveStock === 0
558	              ? 'Out of stock / అయిపోయింది'
559	              : `${liveStock} ${unit} left / మిగిలాయి`}
560	          </p>
561	        )}
562	
563	        {/* Stock warning */}
564	        {stockMsg && (
565	          <p className="text-xs text-amber-700 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-lg px-2 py-1 leading-snug text-center">
566	            {stockMsg}
567	          </p>
568	        )}
569	
570	        {/* CTA */}
571	        {!canAdd ? (
572	          <Link
573	            href={farmerHref}
574	            className="mt-2 block text-center w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
575	          >
576	            View / చూడండి
577	          </Link>
578	        ) : isOutOfStock ? (
579	          <button
580	            disabled
581	            className="mt-2 w-full bg-gray-200 text-gray-500 font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm cursor-not-allowed"
582	          >
583	            Out of stock / అయిపోయింది
584	          </button>
585	        ) : !inCart ? (
586	          <button
587	            onClick={handleAdd}
588	            disabled={adding}
589	            className="mt-2 w-full bg-green-700 active:bg-green-800 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm disabled:opacity-60"
590	          >
591	            {adding ? 'Checking... / తనిఖీ' : '+ Add to cart / చేర్చు'}
592	          </button>
593	        ) : (
594	          <div className="mt-2 flex items-center justify-between bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-xl px-2 py-1.5">
595	            <button
596	              onClick={() => { setQty(item.id, inCart.qty - 1); setStockMsg('') }}
597	              className="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white border border-green-300 text-green-800 text-lg font-bold"
598	              aria-label="Decrease"
599	            >
600	              −
601	            </button>
602	            <span className="font-extrabold text-green-900 text-sm">
603	              {inCart.qty} {unit}
604	            </span>
605	            <button
606	              onClick={handleInc}
607	              disabled={atMax}
608	              className={`w-8 h-8 rounded-lg text-lg font-bold transition-colors ${
609	                atMax
610	                  ? 'bg-gray-200 text-gray-400 cursor-not-allowed'
611	                  : 'bg-green-700 text-white'
612	              }`}
613	              aria-label="Increase"
614	            >
615	              +
616	            </button>
617	          </div>
618	        )}
619	      </div>
620	    </div>
621	  )
622	}
623	
624	/* ─── Coming soon card ──────────────────────────────────── */
625	function ComingSoonCard({ item }: { item: ProduceListing }) {
626	  const emoji   = item.emoji ?? '🌱'
627	  const emojiBg = EMOJI_BG[emoji] ?? 'bg-amber-50'
628	  const farmer  = item.farmer
629	
630	  return (
631	    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border-2 border-dashed border-amber-200 flex flex-col">
632	      <div className={`${emojiBg} flex items-center justify-center py-7`}>
633	        <span className="text-5xl">{emoji}</span>
634	      </div>
635	      <div className="p-3 flex flex-col flex-1 gap-1">
636	        <h3 className="font-extrabold text-gray-700 text-base leading-tight">{item.name}</h3>
637	        {farmer && (
638	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">🧑‍🌾 {farmer.name}</p>
639	        )}
640	        {item.available_to && (
641	          <p className="text-xs text-amber-600 font-medium">
642	            Ready / సిద్ధం: {new Date(item.available_to).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })}
643	          </p>
644	        )}
645	        <span className="mt-1 inline-block bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-full self-start">
646	          Coming soon / త్వరలో
647	        </span>
648	        {farmer && (
649	          <Link
650	            href={`/farmer/${farmer.slug}`}
651	            className="mt-2 block text-center w-full border-2 border-green-600 text-green-700 font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
652	          >
653	            View farmer / రైతు చూడండి
654	          </Link>
655	        )}
656	      </div>
657	    </div>
658	  )
659	}
660	
661	/* ─── Loading skeleton ──────────────────────────────────── */
662	function LoadingSkeleton() {
663	  return (
664	    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
665	      {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((i) => (
666	        <div key={i} className="bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden animate-pulse">
667	          <div className="h-28 bg-gray-100" />
668	          <div className="p-3 space-y-2">
669	            <div className="h-4 bg-gray-100 rounded w-3/4" />
670	            <div className="h-3 bg-gray-100 rounded w-1/2" />
671	            <div className="h-3 bg-gray-100 rounded w-2/3" />
672	            <div className="h-11 bg-gray-100 rounded-xl mt-3" />
673	          </div>
674	        </div>
675	      ))}
676	    </div>
677	  )
678	}
679	
680	/* ─── Distance empty state ──────────────────────────────── */
681	function DistanceEmptyState({ km, onClear }: { km: number; onClear: () => void }) {
682	  return (
683	    <div className="text-center py-14">
684	      <div className="text-6xl mb-4">📍</div>
685	      <p className="text-gray-800 font-bold text-lg">No farmers within {km} km</p>
686	      <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm mt-1">
687	        {km} కి.మీ లోపల రైతులు లేరు
688	      </p>
689	      <p className="text-gray-400 text-xs mt-2 leading-snug px-8">
690	        Farmers nearby may not have set their location yet.<br />
691	        దగ్గరలోని రైతులు ఇంకా లొకేషన్ పెట్టలేదు.
692	      </p>
693	      <button
694	        onClick={onClear}
695	        className="mt-5 bg-green-700 text-white font-bold px-6 py-3 rounded-xl text-sm"
696	      >
697	        Show all produce / అన్ని పంటలు చూపించు
698	      </button>
699	    </div>
700	  )
701	}
702	
703	/* ─── Empty state ───────────────────────────────────────── */
704	function EmptyState() {
705	  return (
706	    <div className="text-center py-14">
707	      <div className="text-6xl mb-4">🔍</div>
708	      <p className="text-gray-800 font-bold text-lg">No produce found / పంట కనుగొనబడలేదు</p>
709	      <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm mt-2">
710	        Try a different search or category<br />
711	        వేరే వెతకండి లేదా వేరే వర్గాన్ని ఎంచుకోండి
712	      </p>
713	    </div>
714	  )
715	}
716	
717	/* ─── Location bottom sheet ─────────────────────────────────── */
718	function LocationBottomSheet({
719	  onSet,
720	  onClose,
721	}: {
722	  onSet: (lat: number, lng: number, name: string) => void
723	  onClose: () => void
724	}) {
725	  const [gpsStatus, setGpsStatus] = useState<'idle' | 'loading' | 'error'>('idle')
726	  const [gpsError, setGpsError]   = useState('')
727	
728	  const handleGPS = () => {
729	    if (!navigator.geolocation) {
730	      setGpsError('GPS not supported on this device / GPS అందుబాటులో లేదు')
731	      return
732	    }
733	    setGpsStatus('loading')
734	    setGpsError('')
735	    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(
736	      (pos) => {
737	        const { latitude, longitude } = pos.coords
738	        const name = nearestTown(latitude, longitude)
739	        onSet(latitude, longitude, name)
740	      },
741	      (err) => {
742	        setGpsStatus('error')
743	        setGpsError(
744	          err.code === 1
745	            ? 'Location access denied. Search your town below. / లొకేషన్ అనుమతి లేదు. పట్టణం వెతకండి.'
746	            : 'Could not get GPS. Search your town below. / GPS రాలేదు. పట్టణం వెతకండి.'
747	        )
748	      },
749	      { timeout: 12000, enableHighAccuracy: true },
750	    )
751	  }
752	
753	  return (
754	    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] bg-black/50 flex items-end justify-center">
755	      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-t-3xl p-5 space-y-4">
756	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
757	          <div>
758	            <h2 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-lg leading-tight">
759	              Where are you? / మీరు ఎక్కడ ఉన్నారు?
760	            </h2>
761	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
762	              See produce from farmers near you / దగ్గరలోని రైతుల పంట చూడండి
763	            </p>
764	          </div>
765	          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 text-3xl leading-none p-1">×</button>
766	        </div>
767	
768	        {/* GPS button */}
769	        <button
770	          onClick={handleGPS}
771	          disabled={gpsStatus === 'loading'}
772	          className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-4 rounded-xl text-base active:bg-green-800 disabled:opacity-60"
773	        >
774	          {gpsStatus === 'loading' ? (
775	            <>
776	              <span className="animate-spin inline-block w-4 h-4 border-2 border-white border-t-transparent rounded-full" />
777	              Detecting... / వెతుకుతోంది
778	            </>
779	          ) : (
780	            <>📍 Use my current location / నా లొకేషన్ వాడండి</>
781	          )}
782	        </button>
783	
784	        {gpsError && (
785	          <p className="text-xs text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 leading-snug">{gpsError}</p>
786	        )}
787	
788	        {/* Divider */}
789	        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
790	          <div className="flex-1 border-t border-gray-200" />
791	          <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-semibold">OR / లేదా</span>
792	          <div className="flex-1 border-t border-gray-200" />
793	        </div>
794	
795	        {/* Search input */}
796	        <div>
797	          <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-2">
798	            Search your location / లొకేషన్ వెతకండి
799	          </label>
800	          <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500 mb-2">
801	            Type any city, town or village in AP / Telangana<br />
802	            ఆంధ్రప్రదేశ్ / తెలంగాణలో ఏ పట్టణమైనా టైప్ చేయండి
803	          </p>
804	          <LocationSearch
805	            placeholder="e.g. Visakhapatnam, Hyderabad, Eluru..."
806	            onSelect={(lat, lng, name) => onSet(lat, lng, name)}
807	          />
808	        </div>
809	
810	        <button
811	          onClick={onClose}
812	          className="w-full text-sm text-gray-500 py-2"
813	        >
814	          Skip for now / తర్వాత చేస్తాను
815	        </button>
816	      </div>
817	    </div>
818	  )
819	}
820	
821	/* ─── Demand intent banner ──────────────────────────────────── */
822	function DemandIntentBanner() {
823	  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
824	  const [crop, setCrop] = useState('')
```

```
33	  const load = useCallback(async () => {

44

46	    setLoading(false)

48	
49	  useEffect(() => { load() }, [load])
50	
51	  const filterStart = () => {
52	    if (filter === 'today') {
53	      const d = new Date()
54	      d.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)
55	      return d.getTime()
56	    }
57	    if (filter === 'week') return Date.now() - 7 * 86400000
58	    return Date.now() - 30 * 86400000
59	  }
60	
61	  const filtered = orders.filter((o) => new Date(o.created_at).getTime() >= filterStart())
62	  const revenue = filtered
63	    .filter((o) => o.status === 'approved')
64	    .reduce((sum, o) => sum + (o.total_price ?? 0), 0)
65	
66	  return (
67	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-16">

74	          {tx.orderHistory}
75	        </h1>
76	        <p className="text-green-400 text-sm mt-1">Approved &amp; declined orders</p>
77	      </div>
78	
79	      <div className="px-4 -mt-5 space-y-4">
80	        {/* Filter pills */}
81	        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-3 flex gap-2">
82	          {(['today', 'week', 'month'] as const).map((f) => (

85	              onClick={() => setFilter(f)}
86	              className={`flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-colors ${
87	                filter === f ? 'bg-green-700 text-white' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 active:bg-gray-200'
88	              }`}
89	            >
90	              {f === 'today' ? tx.filterToday : f === 'week' ? tx.filterWeek : tx.filterMonth}
91	            </button>
92	          ))}
93	        </div>
94	
95	        {/* Revenue summary */}
96	        {revenue > 0 && (
97	          <div className="bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center justify-between">
98	            <div>
99	              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-green-700 uppercase tracking-wide">
100	                {tx.totalRevenue}
101	              </p>
102	              <p className="text-3xl font-black text-green-800 mt-0.5">₹{revenue}</p>
103	            </div>
104	            <div className="text-4xl">💰</div>
105	          </div>
106	        )}
107	
108	        {/* Order list */}
109	        {loading ? (
110	          <div className="text-center py-16">
111	            <div className="w-10 h-10 border-4 border-green-700 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto" />
112	            <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm mt-3">{tx.loadingLabel}</p>
113	          </div>
114	        ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (

123	            ))}
124	          </div>
125	        )}
126	      </div>
127	    </main>

163	            {timeStr}

166	
167	        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 text-sm">
```

```
1	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
2	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	
4	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
5	export const fetchCache = 'force-no-store'
6	
7	export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
8	  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url)
9	  const status = searchParams.get('status') ?? 'available'
10	
11	  const { data: produce, error } = await supabase
12	    .from('produce_listings')
13	    .select('*')
14	    .eq('status', status)
15	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
16	
17	  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })
18	  if (!produce?.length) return NextResponse.json([])
19	
20	  const farmerIds = [...new Set(produce.map((p) => p.farmer_id))]
21	
22	  const { data: farmers } = await supabase
23	    .from('farmers')
24	    .select('id, name, village, slug, phone, method, region_slug, pickup_locations, pickup_slots, lat, lng')
25	    .in('id', farmerIds)
26	    .eq('active', true)
27	
28	  const farmerMap = Object.fromEntries((farmers ?? []).map((f) => [f.id, f]))
29	
30	  const result = produce
31	    .map((p) => ({ ...p, farmer: farmerMap[p.farmer_id] ?? null }))
32	    .filter((p) => p.farmer !== null)
33	
34	  return NextResponse.json(result)
35	}
36	
```

```
106	async function compressImage(file: File, maxPx = 800, quality = 0.7): Promise<File> {
107	  return new Promise((resolve) => {
108	    const img = new Image()
109	    const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file)
110	    img.onload = () => {
111	      URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl)
112	      const scale = Math.min(1, maxPx / Math.max(img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight))
113	      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')
114	      canvas.width = Math.round(img.naturalWidth * scale)
115	      canvas.height = Math.round(img.naturalHeight * scale)
116	      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')
117	      if (!ctx) { resolve(file); return }
118	      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height)
119	      canvas.toBlob(
120	        (blob) => {
121	          if (!blob) { resolve(file); return }
122	          const name = file.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '.jpg')
123	          resolve(new File([blob], name, { type: 'image/jpeg' }))
124	        },
125	        'image/jpeg',
126	        quality,
127	      )
128	    }
129	    img.onerror = () => { URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl); resolve(file) }
130	    img.src = blobUrl
131	  })
132	}
133	
134	const isProfileComplete = (f: Farmer | null) =>
135	  !!f && f.name?.trim().length > 0 && f.village?.trim().length > 0

150	  const [demandBars, setDemandBars] = useState<DemandBar[]>([])
```

```
150	  const [demandBars, setDemandBars] = useState<DemandBar[]>([])
151	  const [monthlyRevenue, setMonthlyRevenue] = useState(0)
152	  const [monthlyOrderCount, setMonthlyOrderCount] = useState(0)
153	  const [weeklyEarnings, setWeeklyEarnings] = useState<number[]>([0, 0, 0, 0])
154	  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false)
155	  const [showProfileEdit, setShowProfileEdit] = useState(false)
156	  const [showListings, setShowListings] = useState(false)
157	
158	  const loadDashboard = useCallback(async () => {
159	    const farmerId = localStorage.getItem('yff_farmer_id')
160	    if (!farmerId) { router.replace('/farmer/login'); return }
161	
162	    const { data: farmerData } = await supabase
163	      .from('farmers')
164	      .select('*')
165	      .eq('id', farmerId)
166	      .maybeSingle()
167	
168	    if (!farmerData) { setNotFound(true); setLoading(false); return }
169	    setFarmer(farmerData)
170	
171	    const monthStart = new Date()
172	    monthStart.setDate(1)
173	    monthStart.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)
174	
175	    const [listingsRes, pendingRes, approvedRes, intentsRes, monthlyRes] = await Promise.all([
176	      supabase.from('produce_listings').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'available'),
177	      supabase.from('orders').select('*').eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'pending').order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
178	      supabase.from('orders').select('id, total_price').eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'approved').gte('created_at', new Date(Date.now() - 7 * 86400000).toISOString()),
179	      supabase.from('demand_intents').select('crop_name, quantity_kg').eq('region_slug', farmerData.region_slug).eq('fulfilled', false),
180	      supabase.from('orders').select('id, total_price, created_at').eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'approved').gte('created_at', monthStart.toISOString()),
181	    ])
182	
183	    setActiveListings(listingsRes.count ?? 0)
184	    setPendingOrders((pendingRes.data ?? []) as Order[])
185	    const approved = approvedRes.data ?? []
186	    setApprovedCount(approved.length)
187	    setTotalRevenue(approved.reduce((sum, o) => sum + (o.total_price ?? 0), 0))
188	
189	    // Monthly earnings
190	    const monthly = monthlyRes.data ?? []
191	    setMonthlyRevenue(monthly.reduce((sum, o) => sum + (o.total_price ?? 0), 0))
192	    setMonthlyOrderCount(monthly.length)
193	
194	    // Break into 4 weekly buckets (days 1-7, 8-14, 15-21, 22+)
195	    const weeks = [0, 0, 0, 0]
196	    for (const o of monthly) {
197	      const day = new Date(o.created_at).getDate()
198	      const bucket = day <= 7 ? 0 : day <= 14 ? 1 : day <= 21 ? 2 : 3
199	      weeks[bucket] += o.total_price ?? 0
200	    }
201	    setWeeklyEarnings(weeks)
202	
203	    const map: Record<string, number> = {}
204	    for (const row of intentsRes.data ?? []) {
205	      map[row.crop_name] = (map[row.crop_name] ?? 0) + (Number(row.quantity_kg) || 0)
206	    }
207	    setDemandBars(
208	      Object.entries(map)
209	        .map(([crop_name, total_qty]) => ({ crop_name, total_qty }))
210	        .sort((a, b) => b.total_qty - a.total_qty)
211	        .slice(0, 5)
212	    )
213	
214	    setLoading(false)
215	  }, [router])
216	
217	  useEffect(() => { loadDashboard() }, [loadDashboard])
218	
219	  // Auto-open the profile edit modal the first time an incomplete farmer lands here.
220	  useEffect(() => {
221	    if (!loading && farmer && !isProfileComplete(farmer)) {
222	      setShowProfileEdit(true)
223	    }
224	  }, [loading, farmer])
225	
226	  const handleLogout = () => {
227	    localStorage.removeItem('yff_farmer_id')
228	    localStorage.removeItem('yff_farmer_slug')
229	    router.replace('/farmer/login')
230	  }
231	
232	  const handleApprove = async (orderId: string) => {
233	    setProcessingOrderId(orderId)
234	    await supabase.from('orders').update({ status: 'approved' }).eq('id', orderId)
235	    setPendingOrders((prev) => prev.filter((o) => o.id !== orderId))
236	    setApprovedCount((c) => c + 1)
237	    setProcessingOrderId(null)
238	  }
239	
240	  const handleDecline = async (orderId: string) => {
241	    setProcessingOrderId(orderId)
242	    await supabase.from('orders').update({ status: 'declined' }).eq('id', orderId)
243	    setPendingOrders((prev) => prev.filter((o) => o.id !== orderId))
244	    setProcessingOrderId(null)
245	  }
246	
247	  const handleMarkPaid = async (orderId: string) => {
248	    setProcessingPaidId(orderId)
249	    await supabase.from('orders').update({ payment_status: 'completed' }).eq('id', orderId)
250	    setPendingOrders((prev) =>
251	      prev.map((o) => o.id === orderId ? { ...o, payment_status: 'completed' } : o)
252	    )
253	    setProcessingPaidId(null)
254	  }
255	
256	  const handleUpdatePaymentStatus = async (orderId: string, status: 'completed' | 'failed' | 'pending') => {
257	    setProcessingPaidId(orderId)
258	    const update: Record<string, string> = { payment_status: status }
259	    if (status === 'completed') update.status = 'approved'
260	    await supabase.from('orders').update(update).eq('id', orderId)
261	    setPendingOrders((prev) =>
262	      prev.map((o) => o.id === orderId ? { ...o, payment_status: status, ...(status === 'completed' ? { status: 'approved' } : {}) } : o)
263	    )
264	    setProcessingPaidId(null)
265	  }
266	
267	  const filteredPendingOrders = pendingOrders.filter((o) => {
268	    const t = new Date(o.created_at).getTime()
269	    const now = Date.now()
270	    if (ordersFilter === 'today') {
271	      const todayStart = new Date()
272	      todayStart.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)
273	      return t >= todayStart.getTime()
274	    }
275	    if (ordersFilter === 'week') return t >= now - 7 * 86400000
276	    return t >= now - 30 * 86400000
277	  })
278	
279	  if (loading) return <LoadingScreen />
280	  if (notFound) return <FarmerNotFound onLogout={handleLogout} />
281	
282	  const profileComplete = isProfileComplete(farmer)
283	  const displayName = farmer!.name?.trim() || tx.welcome
284	
285	  return (
286	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-16">
287	      {/* Header */}
288	      <div className="bg-green-900 px-4 pt-6 pb-10">
289	        <div className="flex justify-end mb-2">
290	          <LanguageToggle />
291	        </div>
292	        <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
293	          <div>
294	            <p className="text-green-400 text-xs font-semibold mb-0.5 uppercase tracking-wide">
295	              {tx.farmerDashboard}
296	            </p>
297	            <h1 className="text-white text-xl font-extrabold leading-tight">{displayName}</h1>
298	            <p className="text-green-300 text-sm mt-0.5">
299	              {profileComplete
300	                ? `${farmer!.village}, ${farmer!.district}`
301	                : tx.completeProfilePrompt}
302	            </p>
303	            <p className="text-green-500 text-xs mt-1">+91 {farmer!.phone}</p>
304	          </div>
305	          <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-2 flex-shrink-0">
306	            {profileComplete ? (

329	      </div>
```

```
537	function ProfileEditModal({

540	  onSaved,
541	}: {

544	  onSaved: (updated: Farmer) => void
545	}) {

553	  )

555	    Array.isArray(farmer.pickup_locations) ? farmer.pickup_locations : [],

559	  // Cover photo

566	  const [avatarPreview, setAvatarPreview] = useState('')
567	  const [existingAvatarUrl, setExistingAvatarUrl] = useState(farmer.photo_url ?? '')
568	
569	  // Pesticide cert
570	  const [certFile, setCertFile] = useState<File | null>(null)
571	  const [certPreview, setCertPreview] = useState('')
572	  const [existingCertUrl, setExistingCertUrl] = useState(farmer.pesticide_cert_url ?? '')
573	
574	  // Pickup schedule

585

592	  const [pwSuccess, setPwSuccess]         = useState(false)
593

596	  const [farmerLng, setFarmerLng] = useState<number | null>(farmer.lng ?? null)
597	  const [farmerLocationName, setFarmerLocationName] = useState(farmer.location_name ?? '')
598	  const [locating, setLocating] = useState(false)
599	  const [locError, setLocError] = useState('')
```

```
604	  const handleFarmerGPS = () => {
605	    if (!navigator.geolocation) {
606	      setLocError('Geolocation not supported on this device.')

612	      (pos) => {
613	        const { latitude, longitude } = pos.coords
614	        setFarmerLat(latitude)
615	        setFarmerLng(longitude)
616	        // Use village name as display name since we have it
617	        setFarmerLocationName(farmer.village || 'Farm')
618	        setLocating(false)
619	      },
620	      (err) => {
621	        setLocError(
622	          err.code === 1
623	            ? 'Location permission denied / లొకేషన్ అనుమతి లేదు. Please allow location in browser settings.'
624	            : 'Could not get location. Please try again. / మళ్ళీ ప్రయత్నించండి.'
625	        )
626	        setLocating(false)
627	      },
628	      { timeout: 15000, enableHighAccuracy: true },
629	    )
630	  }
631	
632	  const handlePickFile = async (
633	    e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>,
634	    setFile: (f: File | null) => void,
635	    setPreview: (s: string) => void,
636	    currentPreview: string,
637	  ) => {
638	    const file = e.target.files?.[0]
639	    if (!file) return
640	    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) { setError(tx.pickImageFile); return }
641	    if (file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) { setError(tx.imageTooLarge); return }
642	    setError('')
643	    if (currentPreview) URL.revokeObjectURL(currentPreview)
644	    const compressed = await compressImage(file)
645	    setFile(compressed)
646	    setPreview(URL.createObjectURL(compressed))
647	  }
648	
649	  const uploadProfileImage = async (file: File, pathSuffix: string): Promise<{ url: string | null; err: string | null }> => {
650	    const ext = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || 'jpg'
651	    // Include timestamp in path so each upload gets a unique URL, busting browser cache
652	    const path = `${farmer.id}/${pathSuffix}-${Date.now()}.${ext}`
653	    const { error: upErr } = await supabase.storage
654	      .from('farm-images')
655	      .upload(path, file, { contentType: file.type, upsert: false })
656	    if (upErr) return { url: null, err: `Upload failed: ${upErr.message}` }
657	    const { data } = supabase.storage.from('farm-images').getPublicUrl(path)
658	    return { url: data.publicUrl, err: null }
659	  }
660	
661	  const toggleDay = (day: string) => {
662	    setSlotDays((prev) =>
663	      prev.includes(day) ? prev.filter((d) => d !== day) : [...prev, day],
664	    )
665	  }
666	
667	  const addPickup = () => {
668	    const v = newPickup.trim()
669	    if (!v) return
670	    if (pickupLocations.includes(v)) { setNewPickup(''); return }
671	    setPickupLocations((prev) => [...prev, v])
672	    setNewPickup('')
673	  }
674	  const removePickup = (loc: string) =>
675	    setPickupLocations((prev) => prev.filter((l) => l !== loc))
676	
677	  const handleSave = async () => {
678	    if (!name.trim()) { setError(tx.nameRequired); return }
679	    if (!village.trim()) { setError(tx.villageRequired); return }
680	    if (upiId.trim() && !/^[a-zA-Z0-9._\-]{2,256}@[a-zA-Z]{2,64}$/.test(upiId.trim())) {
681	      setError('Invalid UPI ID format. Example: yourname@ybl or 9876543210@paytm')
682	      return
683	    }
684	    setLoading(true)
685	    setError('')
686	
687	    // Build unique slug from name if current slug is still auto-generated
688	    const isAutoSlug = /^f-\d{10}-[a-z0-9]{4}$/.test(farmer.slug ?? '')
689	    const baseSlug = name
690	      .toLowerCase()
691	      .trim()
692	      .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
693	      .replace(/(^-|-$)/g, '')
694	      .slice(0, 40) || 'farmer'
695	    let newSlug = farmer.slug
696	    if (isAutoSlug && baseSlug) {
697	      const rand = Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 5)
698	      newSlug = `${baseSlug}-${rand}`
699	    }
700	
701	    // Upload photos in parallel

704	      avatarFile ? uploadProfileImage(avatarFile, 'avatar') : Promise.resolve({ url: null, err: null }),
705	      certFile  ? uploadProfileImage(certFile,  'pesticide-cert') : Promise.resolve({ url: null, err: null }),
706	      qrFile    ? uploadProfileImage(qrFile,    'upi-qr') : Promise.resolve({ url: null, err: null }),
707	    ])
708	    const uploadErr = coverRes.err ?? avatarRes.err ?? certRes.err ?? qrRes.err
709	    if (uploadErr) { setError(uploadErr); setLoading(false); return }
710	
711	    const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
712	      name:             name.trim(),
713	      village:          village.trim(),
714	      district:         district.trim(),
715	      method,
716	      slug:             newSlug,
717	      pickup_locations: pickupLocations,
718	      cover_photo_url:  (coverRes.url ?? existingCoverUrl) || null,
719	      photo_url:        (avatarRes.url ?? existingAvatarUrl) || null,
720	      pesticide_cert_url: (certRes.url ?? existingCertUrl) || null,
721	      upi_id:           upiId.trim() || null,
722	      upi_qr_code_url:  (qrRes.url ?? existingQrUrl) || null,
723	      pickup_slots: slotDays.length > 0
724	        ? { days: slotDays, time_from: slotFrom, time_to: slotTo }
725	        : null,
726	      lat: farmerLat,
727	      lng: farmerLng,
728	      location_name: farmerLat ? (farmerLocationName || name.trim()) : null,
729	    }
730	    if (sinceYear) payload.farming_since_year = Number(sinceYear)
731	
732	    const { data, error: err } = await supabase
733	      .from('farmers')
734	      .update(payload)
735	      .eq('id', farmer.id)
736	      .select('*')
737	      .single()
738	
739	    setLoading(false)
740	
741	    if (err || !data) {
742	      setError(err?.message ?? tx.couldNotSave)
743	      return
744	    }
745	
746	    localStorage.setItem('yff_farmer_slug', data.slug)
747	    onSaved(data)
748	  }
749	
750	  const handleChangePassword = async () => {
751	    if (newPassword.length < 4) { setPwError('Minimum 4 characters / కనీసం 4 అక్షరాలు'); return }
752	    if (newPassword !== confirmPassword) { setPwError('Passwords do not match / పాస్‌వర్డ్‌లు సరిపోలలేదు'); return }
753	    setPwLoading(true)
754	    setPwError('')
```

```
1	-- ================================================================
2	-- YFF Feature Migration
3	-- Run in: Supabase Dashboard → SQL Editor → New Query → Run
4	-- ================================================================
5	
6	-- 1. Harvest date on produce listings
7	ALTER TABLE produce_listings
8	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS harvest_date date;
9	
10	-- 2. Cover photo for farmer profile banner
11	ALTER TABLE farmers
12	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS cover_photo_url text;
13	
14	-- 3. Farmer avatar/profile photo
15	ALTER TABLE farmers
16	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS photo_url text;
17	
18	-- 4. Pesticide test certificate (photo URL)
19	ALTER TABLE farmers
20	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pesticide_cert_url text;
21	
22	-- 5. Pickup schedule: {days: string[], time_from: string, time_to: string}
23	ALTER TABLE farmers
24	  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS pickup_slots jsonb;
25	
26	-- 6. OTP table for secure farmer login
27	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS farmer_otps (
28	  id          uuid        DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,
29	  phone       text        NOT NULL,
30	  otp         text        NOT NULL,
31	  expires_at  timestamptz NOT NULL,
32	  used        boolean     DEFAULT false,
33	  created_at  timestamptz DEFAULT now()
34	);
35	
36	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_farmer_otps_phone   ON farmer_otps(phone);
37	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_farmer_otps_expires ON farmer_otps(expires_at);
38	
39	-- RLS: only service-role key (used in API routes) can access
40	ALTER TABLE farmer_otps ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
41	
42	-- 7. Payment method and status on orders (COD support)
43	ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS payment_method text DEFAULT 'cod';
44	ALTER TABLE orders ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS payment_status text DEFAULT 'pending';
45	
46	-- 8. Farmer GPS location for distance-based discovery
47	ALTER TABLE farmers ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS lat numeric(10,7);
48	ALTER TABLE farmers ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS lng numeric(10,7);
49	ALTER TABLE farmers ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS location_name text;
50	
51	-- 9. Password hash for farmer login
52	ALTER TABLE farmers ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS password_hash text;
53	
54	-- 10. Farm gallery photos
55	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS media (
56	  id          uuid        DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,
57	  farmer_id   uuid        REFERENCES farmers(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
58	  type        text        NOT NULL DEFAULT 'photo',
59	  url         text        NOT NULL,
60	  caption     text,
61	  sort_order  integer     DEFAULT 0,
62	  created_at  timestamptz DEFAULT now()
63	);
64	
65	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_media_farmer_id ON media(farmer_id);
66	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_media_type      ON media(type);
67	
68	-- 11. Reviewer phone for duplicate-review prevention
69	ALTER TABLE reviews ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS reviewer_phone text;
70	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_reviews_farmer_phone ON reviews(farmer_id, reviewer_phone);
71	
72	-- 12. WhatsApp click analytics (insert-only, no auth needed)
73	CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS wa_clicks (
74	  id         uuid        DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,
75	  farmer_id  uuid        REFERENCES farmers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
76	  created_at timestamptz DEFAULT now()
77	);
78	
79	CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_wa_clicks_farmer ON wa_clicks(farmer_id);
80	
81	-- Allow anonymous inserts for wa_clicks (consumer clicks)
82	ALTER TABLE wa_clicks ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
83	DO $$
84	BEGIN
85	  IF NOT EXISTS (
86	    SELECT 1 FROM pg_policies
87	    WHERE tablename = 'wa_clicks' AND policyname = 'anon insert wa_clicks'
88	  ) THEN
89	    EXECUTE 'CREATE POLICY "anon insert wa_clicks" ON wa_clicks FOR INSERT TO anon WITH CHECK (true)';
90	  END IF;
91	END $$;
92
```
